# Die Hard Arcade (Sega ST-V, SH-2 BE) - engine symbol map
# Format: 0x<SH2addr> <name>   # <confidence> <evidence>
# Confidence: HIGH = decompilation-proven; MED = strong structural/HW evidence; GUESS = plausible, unverified.
# SH2 addresses (cart base 0x02200000). Many runtime routines live in HWRAM 0x06000000 (copied at boot) and are NOT in this image.

# ---- math / core ----
0x02210020 rng_next            # HIGH  PRNG: seed@0x0609957c, XOR-feedback const 0x2a6d365b, then multiply-mix
0x022131c8 fixmul_16_16        # HIGH  signed 16.16 fixed-point multiply (64-bit mul >>16)
0x02213234 sh2_hw_divide       # HIGH  SH-2 on-chip DIVU 64/32 (DVSR/DVDNTH/DVDNTL @0xffffff00, reads DVDNTUL)
0x02213190 sin_lookup          # HIGH  trig table @0x06075718, idx=(ang+8)>>4, result<<1
0x022131a8 cos_lookup          # HIGH  same table @0x06075718 + 0x800 offset
0x022131d4 dist2_xz_fixed      # HIGH  squared distance on x/z, 16.16 (dx*dx+dz*dz)
0x022132c4 vec3_dot_fixed      # HIGH  3-vector dot product, 16.16, saturating (uses MAC S-bit)
0x02213ca4 vec3_array_matmul   # MED   matrix*vector over an array, 16.16 saturating (3D transform)
0x02213474 matrix_copy_reverse # MED   reversed 12-word (3x4 matrix) block copy

# ---- memory / DMA (no classic byte memcpy in-cart; bulk via DMA) ----
0x02213290 clear_vec4_array    # HIGH  zero-fill N x 16-byte elements (memset 0)
0x022132ac fill_vec4_array     # HIGH  fill N x 16-byte elements with a value (memset)
0x022148ac zero_fill_aligned   # MED   aligned word zero-fill of a region
0x022145d0 sh2_dmac_copy       # HIGH  SH-2 on-chip DMAC ch0 (SAR/DAR/TCR/CHCR @0xffffff80-8c, DMAOR @0xffffffb0)
0x0221478a scu_dma0_transfer   # HIGH  SCU DMA level0 (D0R/D0W/D0C/D0MD @0x25fe0000..14)
0x0221482e scu_dma_push        # MED   SCU DMA, descriptor/stack-fed variant
0x02214bb8 alloc_array_zeroed  # MED   allocate count*size then clear

# ---- alloc / spawn / task-actor system ----
0x0220feec spawn_obj_a         # MED   allocate + init object (size from pool), zero-init
0x0220fece spawn_obj_0x6c      # MED   allocate + init 0x6c-byte object
0x0220ff28 spawn_obj_0x7c      # MED   allocate + init 0x7c-byte object
0x0222a5d0 process_spawn_list  # MED   walk spawn script (0x18-byte entries), create+init entities
0x02244e2e actor_state_dispatch# MED   call current-state handler via function-pointer table (per actor)
0x0222dc2c animation_update    # MED   large per-actor animation / frame-advance routine
0x02211108 actor_update_thrown # MED   entity update: state machine (init/move/physics+gravity+bounce/death)
0x0220f816 anim_set_state      # MED   set entity animation state from anim-data record
0x022470a2 actor_set_state_0x12# GUESS set actor state field = 0x12 + flags
0x0224840a actor_set_state_7   # GUESS set actor state field = 7 + flags

# ---- input (SMPC @0x20100001.. / cmd regs) ----
0x0220acf6 smpc_intback_read   # HIGH  SMPC INTBACK (COMREG=0x10): issue, wait SF, read OREG>>4 (peripheral/status)
0x02214918 smpc_cmd_sshoff     # HIGH  SMPC COMREG=0x03 (SSHOFF, slave SH-2 off)
0x02214944 smpc_cmd_sshon      # HIGH  SMPC COMREG=0x02 (SSHON, slave SH-2 on)
0x02214970 smpc_pdr2_set       # MED   SMPC PDR2 @0x20100077 bit set (port/GPIO)
0x02214980 smpc_pdr2_clr       # MED   SMPC PDR2 @0x20100077 bit clear

# ---- sound (SCSP / sound RAM) ----
0x02215da8 sound_init          # HIGH  load sound driver into sound RAM (0x25a00000) + poke SCSP ctrl (0x25b00400)
0x02215e20 sound_send_command  # HIGH  copy command block into sound-RAM command area, trigger driver
0x02215f6c sound_queue_push    # HIGH  push request into 16-entry ring (idx@0x060e0dec, base@0x060e0df0)
0x02215f36 sound_queue_service # HIGH  dequeue ring (idx@0x060e0de8) -> active sound command

# ---- VDP1 (sprites / polygons) ----
0x02223cf0 isr_vblank_in       # HIGH  SCU VBlank-IN vector(0x40) ISR (installed by main_init_entry); per-vblank VDP1 framebuffer/mode (TVMR/FBCR). [was vdp1_set_mode]
0x02223d98 isr_vblank_out      # HIGH  SCU VBlank-OUT vector(0x41) ISR; increments frame counter (via DAT_02223e1c), commits VDP regs. [was vdp1_fb_flip]
0x02214cdc vdp1_frame_begin    # HIGH  PTMR/EWDR/EWLR/EWRR (@0x25d00004..0a) erase+plot setup
0x02214d26 vdp1_draw_kick      # HIGH  set PTMR, wait draw-end status, push cmd (indeg 13)
0x02214dae vdp1_set_erase      # HIGH  set erase-area coordinate registers
0x02214f90 vdp1_build_cmdlist  # HIGH  walk 0x40 lists, DMA 0x20-byte cmds into VDP1 VRAM (0x25c00000)
0x022150d4 vdp1_add_sprite_cmd # MED   build a VDP1 command-table entry from a short* descriptor

# ---- VDP2 (scroll backgrounds) ----
0x02202138 vdp2_scene_setup    # MED   large VDP2 bg/mode init, state-driven (TVMD/scroll regs @0x25f80000, CRAM)
0x02201c9c vdp2_update_a       # MED   VDP2 register update (state-driven)
0x02201f20 vdp2_update_b       # MED   VDP2 register update
0x02223dca isr_timer0          # HIGH  SCU Timer-0 vector(0x43) ISR; cycles 4-entry display page (idx+1&3), toggles VDP2 reg. [was display_page_cycle]

# ---- SCU / timer / executive ----
0x022013c4 frame_sync_loop     # HIGH  in-cart per-frame executive; entered via tail-bra from main_init_entry; pushes display cmds (5,7), waits SCU T0C status @0x25fe0090; dispatches HWRAM tick via PIC thunk + the per-frame pipeline below
0x0223e5cc scu_timer0_set      # GUESS write SCU Timer0 compare (@0x25fe0090)

# ---- interrupt / vblank  (RESOLVED: ISRs registered by main_init_entry via BIOS set-func @ *0x06000300) ----
0x02201020 main_init_entry     # HIGH  reset init -> main: clears globals (FUN_02201958), installs ISRs for SCU vec 0x40/0x41/0x43, programs SCU Timer0 (T1MD@0x25fe0098=1, T0C@0x25fe0090=0x3ff), unmasks IRQs (SR I=0), tail-bra frame_sync_loop. SCU int-mask @0x25fe00a0 is handled by the BIOS set-func routine (never written by a cart literal).

# ---- per-frame executive pipeline (called from frame_sync_loop via PIC thunks) ----
0x0220fde4 task_list_run       # HIGH  walk active-task linked list; call each node's handler fn-ptr (*node); free dead nodes to freelist; store active count @DAT_0220feac
0x0222ece4 collision_grid_pass # MED   per-frame spatial-grid (0x20 x 0x10 bins) pairwise collision pass (FUN_0222ef38/FUN_0222f1d0)
0x022225f6 sprite_transform_setup # MED  per-frame sprite affine setup: sin/cos of entity angles (+0x24/+0x26) -> 2x2 matrix fields (+0x1c..+0x30)
0x022227c4 displist_priority_fill # MED  per-frame display-list priority/depth byte fill (value <<0x18 into command entries)
0x02223ee0 actor_behavior_fsm  # MED   large per-actor AI/behavior state machine (7610B, braf jumptable on state@entity+DAT_02223ffe); reached via fn-ptr tables, not the global game mode

# ---- high in-degree dispatchers (32-way braf value/operand decoders) ----
0x0224a360 dispatch_table_a    # MED   32-way computed-goto (braf) dispatch on r1 index 0..0x1f via byte table @DAT_0224a380; r0 operand in, value out; used as a value/operand generator (e.g. FUN_0221279c calls it 9x for pos/vel/angle deltas)
0x0224a420 dispatch_table_b    # MED   signed variant of _a: handles r0 sign bit, rebases index by -0x18 for high range, returns -1/0 out-of-range; feeds sprite-attribute field values (e.g. FUN_0222156c)

# ---- glib thunks into HWRAM-resident library ----
0x02214a2e matrix_stack_push   # MED   reserve 0x30 on a stack (*ptr+=0x30), copy 0xc, call HWRAM routine

# ---- functional batch (Opus) : named leaf/formatter routines ----
0x0220074c crc16_buffer       # HIGH  CRC-16 over [buf,len); seed 0xDEBDEB00, poly 0x1021 in high half, returns high 16 bits
0x0222378c fmt_uint_base2     # HIGH  right-justified binary number formatter into an output ctx (pad/width/cursor/count); glyphs @0x060755FB
0x02223968 fmt_uint_base16    # HIGH  right-justified hex formatter; glyph table @0x060755FE (A) / 0x0607560F (B), selected by arg

# ---- functional batch 2 (Opus) : braf-resolver unlocks + copy routines ----
# (braf/bsrf PIC tail targets resolved with tools/decomp/braf_resolve.py: target = braf_addr+4+pool_delta)
0x02223dca isr_timer0_confirm  # HIGH  isr_timer0 body decompiled; tail-branches (braf) to sound_queue_service @0x02215f36 each timer tick (resolver-validated against the named symbol)
0x0214e2e  entity_radix_sort   # HIGH  3-pass LSD radix sort of 0x24-byte entity records by the 24-bit key@+0x20 into 0x100-way linked-list buckets (b1@0x060BACC0/arena 0x060BB8C0, b2@0x060BF4C0/arena 0x060C00C0; 0xC-byte nodes {next,entity,key}); sorts two entity arrays into draw order
0x0222eea2 drawlist_prepend    # HIGH  prepend an entity to a 32-entry list: node=&g_06097450[count*8], node{next@0=head,entity@4}; count@0x06097550 (cap 32), head@0x06097554
0x022227c4 displist_priority_fill # HIGH (was MED) OR a 7-bit priority ((g_060BACA8-((word&0x7FFFF)>>15)+0xF)&0x7F)<<24 into the top byte of 0xF0 display-list words; mode 0/1 = list A@*0x060BAC98 / B@*0x060BAC9C self-keyed, mode 2 = merge A else B; gated by g_060BACB4&1

# ---- functional batch 3 (Opus) : braf/bsrf bulk-rewrite wrapper unlocks the actor-handler family ----
# (tools/decomp/braf_rewrite.py rewrites m2c_fn.py asm: braf Rn -> bsr func_<t>; rts/nop (tail);
#  bsrf Rn -> bsr func_<t> (call); delay slots hoisted. Targets from braf_resolve.py.)
0x0222adaa actor_pose_dispatch   # MED  shared actor continuation (braf->0x22adaa family): dispatches pose fn from table g_060911BC[g_0609979A](entity,unk64), runs func_0222aadc, resets unk80/7C/48/52 + unk41=1, ticks a >0x1E staleness timer on unk110/unk10C vs frame counter g_060987BC, tail-dispatches per-type handler g_060911C4[entity->unkBA&0x3F](entity,&g_060987BC)
0x0222dc2c animation_update       # HIGH (was MED) confirmed 2-arg animation_update(ctx_r4, entity_r5); returns a trigger-frame flag (r0); tail/called by the whole 0x228xxx-0x229xxx actor state-step family
# Actor state-step handlers (entity=r5, ctx=r4; each runs/branches around animation_update then a shared continuation). Named generically func_0222xxxx; roles are per-state micro-handlers:
0x02228508 actor_step_228508      # MED  task-handler form (entity=task->unk8): sub-update + animation_update + func_0222ca98, advances task->unk0 to func_0222863e
0x02228a0e actor_step_gravity     # MED  gravity/landing arc on unkC4/C8/7C; on land resets slot g_06096C74[unk34] + picks landing anim
0x022289b8 actor_step_launch      # MED  on animation_update trigger, arms a launch arc (unkC4/C8/78, anim 0x143/0x145 by facing)

# ---- functional batch 4 (Opus) : shared callees + boot/camera runway ----
# Shared callees (sharpen the 0x228xxx-0x229xxx actor state-step family named in batch 3):
0x0222c220 actor_ledge_fall_gate    # MED  gates+arms a fall/launch arc from per-zone record g_06098734[unk34-1]; writes unkC4/C8/84/88 arc fields; returns -1 armed / 0 gated. Called by 0x22881a,0x2288e8,...
0x02227704 actor_facing_resolve     # MED  matrix-transforms per-actor record g_06096328[unk34] (push/pop stack @g_06098878), sets facing unk26=0x4000/0xC000 + flip flag unk44 from transformed-X sign g_06098B24
0x0223a6dc actor_pose_load          # HIGH loads pose record g_0608CE8C[unk6A] (8B stride) -> unk5C (anim id) / unk90 (frames); forces unk84=0x400000 for player slots (unk34<3). Called by 0x2288e8 etc.
0x0222aadc actor_sound_emit         # MED  for type-0x14 (unkBA&0x3F) or no-hit-timer (unk56==0) actors, pushes (g_06087EE4[unk34-1]<<8)|0xA00000FF via sound_queue_push. Run by actor_pose_dispatch (0x22adaa)
0x0223a394 actor_queue_mark         # HIGH one-liner: g_060E0F2C[unk94] = 1 (mark command-queue slot)
0x0223b1c0 actor_state_gen_grab     # MED  grab/hold state generator; restores partner anim, nudges partner facing; returns 1 (caller 0x229816 does state = ret + 0x21 = 0x22)
# Larger anim-family members (task-form; braf_rewrite.py + per-bsrf arg transcription):
0x0222a970 actor_anim_init_task     # MED  task-form anim init (task->unk8=entity): loads unkD0 anim rec, zeroes affine fields, primes unkC4 timer, installs func_0222a9fe as task->unk0 (next state)
0x0222a9fe actor_anim_step_task     # MED  task-form anim frame step: decrement unkC4, advance anim-script ptr unkD0 on expiry, transition task->unk0 to func_0222a5ca on end (unk3C==0x10)
# Boot / init:
0x02201958 boot_globals_init        # HIGH (was FUN_02201958, called by main_init_entry) clears game-state HWRAM globals to reset values; probes machine via BIOS callbacks *0x0600063C(3,&g_06098674) and *0x06000634(&g_060987EC) + smpc_intback_read; decodes region/config (g_06000248, INTBACK buf) -> language g_06099794 / players g_06099796; sets sound-enable g_060E0DDC=1
# Runway (task handlers):
0x0220b354 task_entity_trampoline   # MED  task handler: runs func_0220d8d0(task,0), then calls entity's own handler (entity->unk64) with task, re-installs self as task->unk0
0x022029a4 hud_number_draw_task     # MED  task handler: draws a fixed-point value (entity->unk60, start 0x10000, +=0x60/frame) at screen (0x160,0x78) via func_02221460
0x02202c5c scene_hud_setup_task     # MED  task handler: scene/HUD init; func_0220f990 + func_0223dcc2(1/2), mirrors language g_06099794, spawns 0x6C obj (template@0x0220340C), -> func_02202dfa
0x0220d262 actor_orbit_pos_task     # MED  task handler: orbit position update - angle unk74+=unk70, pos = 0x30000*{sin,cos}(angle)+base; feeds func_0221055c
0x0220cfac camera_follow_update     # HIGH task handler: camera follow of active player slot (unkB0) + dynamic zoom (sums per-slot proximity/state over 8 slots, clamp 0x20000) + orbit (sin/cos) + eased position; sets roll/yaw unk24/26
0x0221055c sprite_transform_build   # GUESS 8-arg (x,y,z, bx,by,bz, &ang, &ang2) sprite/transform build; called by actor_orbit_pos_task
# Generator family (identified, .c deferred - heavy PIC data-pointer / fn-ptr-table args):
0x02239fc6 actor_state_gen_main     # MED  large state generator, switch on unk88 (cases 0x4F-0x53); returns a small int used as state = ret + 0x21 (caller 0x229644)
0x0223a3a2 actor_state_release_gen  # MED  paired release/transition state generator (nested switch on unk6C/unk6D); called by 0x22979c
0x0223ac9c actor_state_gen_special  # MED  special-move state generator (switch on unk6D, spawns effect obj); tail target of handler 0x229854
0x0223b01e actor_state_gen_throw    # MED  throw/spawn state generator; allocates obj, returns 6 (caller 0x2297d8 does state = ret + 0x21)

# ---- functional batch 5 (Opus) : PIC data-pointer resolver unlocks generators + boot/VDP2 ----
# NEW TOOL tools/decomp/ptr_resolve.py : resolves PIC mova/add DATA pointers (spawn templates,
#   fn-ptr-table bases, next-state ptrs). target = mova_anchor_addr + pool_delta (SH2 = +0x02000000).
#   Validated: 0x22a970->0x0222a9fe, 0x22a9fe->0x0222a5ca, 0x202c5c->0x0220340C (batch-4 hand cases).
0x02239fc6 actor_state_gen_main      # HIGH (was MED) DECOMPILED: main combat-state generator; switch on unk88 (0x4F-0x53) swaps saved anim words g_060E11E8[unk34]<->g_06096248[unk34], installs action into grab slots unk6C/6D/6E/6F, updates flag mask unk8C from g_0604EBE4[cmd]; shared tail force-releases stuck grab + restores hit-anim; returns small int (caller 0x2229644 does state=ret+0x21). cmd = g_060E0F0C[unk94]; clears mark g_060E0F2C[unk94] (inverse of actor_queue_mark)
0x0223b01e actor_state_gen_throw     # HIGH (was MED) DECOMPILED: throw/projectile spawn generator; spawn_obj_a(g_06099580, template@0x0223D1B6 [ptr_resolve]); seeds projectile pos from g_06097D88[unk34]; validates via func_0223cbb0, returns 6 (caller 0x22297d8 -> state 0x27)
0x02229488 actor_hit_state_step      # HIGH DECOMPILED: actor hit/grab state handler (switch on unk88 0x45-0x49); 0x47 spawns partner/effect via func_0220ff72(g_06099580, template@0x02226EB8 [ptr_resolve]) and links spawned->unk8->unk10/14; tails func_02216110/animation_update/actor_pose_dispatch
0x0223b254 actor_hit_anim_apply      # GUESS called by actor_state_gen_main default; indexes per-action table by entity->unk6D, writes actor record (real function: 0x23b1c0 ends exactly here; not fully decompiled)
# Boot / VDP2 cluster (port-relevant init):
0x02201b8e noop_handler              # HIGH DECOMPILED: empty rts;nop. Shared default/no-op callback - its address is installed as a default fn-ptr slot by func_022016ac (x3) and func_02201b6c
0x02201b92 boot_input_region_init    # HIGH DECOMPILED: sibling of boot_globals_init; inits sound, region gate (g_060511DC), reads peripheral via BIOS *0x06000660, controller-type flag g_06098668, language g_06099794=g_060511D2, config bytes g_060987EC[g_06098808], sound-enable g_060E0DDC. Called by func_02016a0
0x02201c9c vdp2_update_a             # HIGH (was MED) DECOMPILED = screen_fade_sequencer: CRAM/palette fade; ramps fade level g_06098668 2..10, palette base g_06074000+level*0x50 applied via func_0220fece (or BIOS *0x0600060C); on completion enables VDP2 display (TVMD 0x25F80000) + sound. func_0220fdc8 ptr via ptr_resolve (mova 0x201cb4+0xE114)
0x02201f20 vdp2_update_b             # HIGH (was MED) DECOMPILED = scene_display_setup_seq: 3-state (task->unk10) VDP2 shadow-register (*g_06098F08) setup; state0 full re-init + subsystem kicks, state1 countdown->display on (TVMD), state2 tail func_0220fdc8

# ---- functional batch 6 (Opus) : the "GBR entity-base" problem was a MISDIAGNOSIS ----
# There is NO GBR entity base. The 0x228xxx-0x229xxx actor state-step handlers reach the entity via
# the r5 argument (moved to r14), using the ordinary SHC @(r0,Rn) field idiom (r0 = mov #imm). The
# bogus "0x4F22 / -0x2D3C / entity-base-lost" constants batch 5 saw come from feeding m2c a
# POOL-CONTAMINATED start address: fn.units() occasionally reports a unit's first member a few words
# INSIDE the previous function's trailing literal pool (the grouper stops absorbing pool early), so
# capstone disassembles pool-as-code (-0x2D3C is just the spawn-template delta 0x228e84-0x226148).
# NEW TOOL tools/decomp/find_entry.py : returns a function's REAL entry. A contaminated address
#   disassembles as capstone `.byte` skipdata and/or sits in fn.all_pool(); the real entry is then the
#   previous function's extent-end. Verified: 0x2296da->0x229714, 0x228d76->0x228d9c,
#   0x228efc->0x228f08, 0x22812e->0x228134. Also surfaced the whole 0x228-0x229 band is ALREADY done
#   (prior batch wrote files under SH2-address names 022xxxxx.c; scan_family's done-check missed them).
# NEW TOOL tools/decomp/decomp_bounded.py : decompile with an explicit --end (clamps fn.extent at
#   runtime; never edits the read-only sources) AND tailifies a direct `bra` to an out-of-window
#   address into a tail call. This is the 0x23ac9c unlock (its rts is at 0x23b01a but it also tail-bras
#   to the shared 0x23b254 OVER the intervening TUs 0x23b01e/0x23b1c0).

# Genuinely-new functional .c this batch (2 from the GBR band, 12 fresh leaves/near-leaves):
0x0222863e actor_task_main_step     # HIGH task handler (task->unk8=entity) installed by func_02228508 as task->unk0; dispatches entity->unk43 handler via fn-ptr table g_06091100[](task,entity), ticks hit-shake timer unk4E (unk20=(frame&1)<<8; clears unkBC/unk20 on 0), runs func_0222ca98/0222eee4/0223df30, publishes screen pos from per-zone rec g_06098734[slot-1] (0x18) to g_0609979C/g_060997A0
0x02228e0c actor_step_grab_follow   # HIGH sibling of func_02228e60 (HIDDEN by fn.extent over-run of 0x228d9c; recovered via find_entry.py). animation_update; if unk54&2 set unk43=0x13; ease paired obj g_060995B0[unk4B]->unk8 pos (unk8/unk10) halfway toward entity unk8+unkC4 / unk10+unkC8
0x022131fc dist2_xyz_fixed          # HIGH 3-axis squared distance in 16.16 (dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz on unk0/4/8); 3D sibling of dist2_xz_fixed 0x022131d4
0x022100a2 str_to_hw_table          # HIGH write a NUL-terminated byte string into g_060997B0 halfword table at row*0x80+col*2, OR-ing each byte with a flag word; NUL not stored
0x02216018 sound_emit_latch         # HIGH if g_060E0DDC (sound enable): sound_queue_push((id<<8)|0xA01000FF); always latches g_060E0DE0=id
0x0221603e sound_emit_gated         # HIGH if g_06053DBC[g_06098668*4+1]==0: sound_queue_push((id<<8)|0xA00300FF)
0x02216110 actor_sound_emit_slot    # HIGH if g_060E0DDC: sound_queue_push((g_060542E8[unk34-1]<<8)|0xA00000FF); per-actor sound. Called by the actor state-step family (e.g. func_02229488)
0x0222f3c0 actor_pair_propagate     # HIGH slot=unk34; if g_06099590[slot]!=0: g_060995B0[slot]->unk8->unkB0 = entity->unk5D
0x0222a91a actor_level_band         # HIGH unk4F = (g_06098668<0xE)?0:(g_06098668<0x27)?1:2; forced 2 if g_060987C8==1
0x0221707c actor_slot_record_reg    # HIGH if unk34<0x20: fill g_060B6D9C[unk34] (0x48 stride) {unk4=slot, unk18/1C/20/24=args1-4(a4 on stack), unk8=entity}
# (.c files also written this batch for already-NAMED symbols: 0x022131d4 dist2_xz_fixed,
#  0x02214918 smpc_cmd_sshoff, 0x02214944 smpc_cmd_sshon, 0x02214dae [= VDP1 cmd-list terminate
#  0x8000@0x25C00000 + reset radix-sort arena ptrs g_060BACB8=0x060BB8C0 / g_060BACBC=0x060C00C0;
#  the "vdp1_set_erase" label is narrower than the body].)

# ---- functional batch 7 (Opus) : done-check normalization + port-primitive leaf mop-up ----
# CONVENTION FIX: the done-check now normalizes filenames by MEMBERSHIP test (not a
#   >=0x02000000 heuristic, which mis-maps low SH2 addresses like boot 0x0201b8e onto
#   in-universe cart 0x201b8e). A file names member cart C iff int(stem)==C or
#   int(stem)==C+0x02000000; else it is out-of-universe (boot cluster / find_entry /
#   decomp_bounded TU) and counted as an "extra". Canonical form = SH2-address 8-char
#   (022xxxxx.c). Fixed in tools/decomp/scan_family.py + project/progress_functional.py
#   (both dedup by canonical address). Renamed the one ambiguous boot file
#   0201b8e.c -> 02001b8e.c (SH2 0x0201b8e, cart 0x1b8e, below code_start) so it stops

# --- Task 2b: ptr_resolve.py boot-cluster halfword swap (tool extension) ---
# The 0x200xxx boot pools sit at 2-aligned offsets, so a 4-aligned mov.l/mova read grabs a
# longword straddling two constants -> the running value is swap16(raw). pool_read.py already
# did this for mov.l pools + bsrf/braf deltas; ptr_resolve.py now does it for mova+add DATA/
# FUNCTION-pointer deltas too, GATED to start in BOOT_LO..BOOT_HI (0x200400..0x201020) so every
# non-boot resolution (0x22xxxx ...) is byte-for-byte unchanged. Within the cluster it reports
# raw + swapped targets and recommends whichever lands in a valid address window (pool_read
# classify); both/neither -> keep raw + flag. Validated on 0x200668:
#   add@200680 raw->0xe7b40680(invalid) / swap->0x0220ec14(ROM) => SWAPPED (confirms the bsrf
#     sibling at 200684 -> 0x20ec1c; the fn-ptr is that callee's alt-entry 0x20ec14)
#   add@200694 raw->0x02505bd6(ROM, valid) / swap->invalid => RAW  (pick is per-constant)
#
# --- RESISTED / deferred (honest) ---
# * 0x200668-class boot BRANCH leaves (0x200668/0x200cd0/0x200400/0x200d46 ...): bsrf deltas now
#   swap-resolve (pool_read) and the mova+add fn-ptrs resolve (ptr_resolve), BUT the callees
#   (0x20eade, 0x20ec1c/0x20ec14) are jump-table dispatchers using the same misaligned-pool idiom,
#   AND func_020eade reads r7 which 0x200668 never sets (implicit register pass-through -> the
#   leaf's own ABI is >4 regs and unconfirmed). Below the verified bar; tools are ready for a
#   focused boot pass once the eade/ec1c jump tables are mapped.
# * func_0220d8d0 (task trampoline callee) and f_220fc82(void*,s32) are called faithfully but the
#   former is still undecompiled.
# * Untouched per constraints: 0x24axxx dispatch band (the pure-leaf scan surfaced 0x24a39c/4aa..
#   /4c0../ab5c.. etc. - all skipped), 0x2896xx data-as-code (0x28966a.. etc. - skipped),
#   confirmed misseg (0x268420/0x2644d6 disassemble as garbage - not real functions), the
#   non-standard-ABI 0x221354/0x221384, port/ + tools/saturn-recomp/, read-only m2c_fn/fn/match.

# --- Batch 13 RESISTED / deferred (honest) ---
# * 0x0222db44 - DEFERRED. Makes three calls into the 0x24Axxx runtime band through non-standard
#   register ABI (r0/r1/r2): bsrf->0x24A360 (a 32-way byte-offset-table dispatcher, index r1) and
#   two bsrf->0x24A280 (dispatch-or-variable-block-copy, selector r0=108 -> copy path, byte count
#   not pinnable from the bounded window). Not faithfully expressible as standard C without
#   establishing each helper's exact ABI/semantics; matches the table-dispatch / ABI-unconfirmed
#   defer rules.
# * 0x24A120/0x24A280/0x24A360 themselves - compiler/runtime helpers (unsigned divide; copy/
#   dispatch; table dispatch). Left in the 0x24Axxx band per constraints; called as externs only
#   where ABI is standard (0x24A120 modeled as unsigned division in resident_query_seq).
# * Larger in-band candidate 0x0222ab18 (0x148) - unk56-decrement + late-join trigger kin to
#   0x22ac60 but with more guard conditions and multiple func_02210020 calls; not traced this
#   batch (budget/quality). Clean next-batch target.
